# Bonk Quest

The game was called DiceBattles until 2026-10-17 (the dice duel it was named after is gone); the user renamed it **Bonk Quest** (logo A "Bonk Burst", art in `art/branding/`).

A Roblox game. Code is synced into Studio by Rojo; the map, UI and models are built live in Studio through the Roblox Studio MCP tools and in Blender through the Blender MCP.

## Where things live (folder layout since 2026-10-17)

```
C:\FirstRobloxGame\           the git repo (GitHub joeycros13/BonkQuest), branch main
  CLAUDE.md  README.md  default.project.json  aftman.toml  .gitignore  .gitattributes
  src\        game code: shared\ server\ client\  (Rojo syncs it into Studio)
  art\        meshes\ (FBX + offsets JSON)  icons\ (weapons, materials, armor, cores)
              ui\ (hud, inventory, shop, quest, index, tiles, buttons, admin, leaderboard...)
              portraits\ (quest, enemies, worlds)  tools\ (the Python/HTML/SVG generators)
  blender\    models.blend            NOT in git; backed up to Google Drive
  place\      BonkQuest.rbxlx       a local backup of the place (the real one is on Roblox since 2026-10-23); NOT in git; Google Drive
  .claude\worktrees\   one per Claude session (managed by the app; not in git)
```

- Older notes below name files by their pre-2026-10-17 paths (`art/meshes/forest_w2.fbx`, `art/index/…`, `art/hud_buttons/…`). The FBX names follow the 2026-10-13 rename (`w2_forest.fbx`…; map in the `blender-modeling` skill), portraits are in `art/portraits/`, UI art in `art/ui/<area>/`, and generators in `art/tools/`. A full copy of the old layout is in `C:\Backups\DiceBattles_2026-10-17`.
- **`art\` holds only live art (the user, 2026-10-20: "all old art can be deleted").** Every PNG is the source of an image the game uses now; the rest is the branding (game icon and thumbnail, for publishing), the FBX exports with their offsets, and the generator scripts in `art	ools\` (Python / HTML / SVG that draw the icons and UI skins, so one can be redrawn or recoloured, plus `svg_render.py`, `ui_center_check.py`, the portrait keyers and the balance simulation). When an image is replaced, delete the old file: no `_older` folders, unchosen options, raw renders or review sheets (git history keeps them). `art	oolsrt_audit.py` matches every file against the thumbnails of the images the place and `src` use; on 2026-10-20 it found 223 of 225 live (the other two are the branding) after 201 old files were removed.
- Never commit `place/` or `blender/` (the place is about 130 MB, over GitHub's 100 MB limit).
- This file is committed, so every session and worktree sees the same copy: update it on your branch and merge.

## The map was moved: World 1's spawn is at x 0, z 0 (2026-10-20)

The user had the whole map moved so World 1's `Spawns/PlayerSpawn` sits at x 0, z 0: everything in `Workspace.Worlds` and `Workspace.Shared` moved **z −204** (x and y unchanged; 204 is a multiple of 4, so the 4-stud ground grids still line up). **Every coordinate in these notes, the skills and the memories written before this point is pre-shift: subtract 204 from its z** (e.g. World 2's gate z −290 → −494, its arena centre (68, −728) → (68, −932)).
- **Moved with it:** the world-position attributes `BasePivot`, `LookAt` (telescope), `Origin`, `PrePosition2026_10_17`, `PreRimFixPivot`, `_OldPivot`; the dev tools' hard-coded z (`BlockRegen` R.Z0/Z1 −498..30, `Replant` region −496..28 and the boss sightline −369..−418 / −406, `W2Dress` keep-out zones and sampling range −1010..−500).
- **Not moved:** backups in ServerStorage (their `WasAt` etc. are pre-shift), the `_W3Grid` dev data, the balance dumps `art/tools/balance/world*.json` (only relative distances matter there), and `NextAt` on World 3's track pieces (a relative offset an earlier move shifted by mistake; dev-only).
- **The secret rooms are far out in the sky (2026-10-20, the user: not under the map):** the Corn Wraith cellar 2,000 studs west of World 1 (centre (−2000, −86, −218.5)) and the Fairy's stump room 2,000 studs east of World 2 (centre (2000, −78, −776)), floors still at y −100, each moved with its `Ground` floor and its enemy's marker; the silo drop, the fairy ring and both exits work unchanged (their targets are the rooms' own parts). Their `Ground` folders carry a `Secret` attribute, so `WorldBannerUI` leaves them out of the world footprints (a player in one has no world; `CurrentWorld` keeps the last one).
- **The stump room is as big as the cellar:** the 16 wall panels moved 4 studs out (inner radius 16 → 20, 40 across) and widened by 1.25 so the ring stays closed (rays all round: radius 19.9..20.4, no gaps); the roof lid, rings and floor grew 4 in radius; the exit arch, moss and mushrooms by the walls moved out 4; the `TwilightZone` and firefly box grew 8; the fairy spot, glow and arrival stayed. **The Fairy is inside again:** World 2's stretch had moved its marker `Fairy_01` 26 studs out of the room (the room wasn't mapped); it now stands on `FairySpot` facing the arrival. Backups `MapBackup_2026-10-20/W2StumpRoomBefore` / `W2StumpRoomFloorBefore` (untagged).
- **The telescope's view can't be blocked (the user's pick):** while looking, `TelescopeController` hides (LocalTransparencyModifier, this player only) every decoration and gathering node of its world whose bounding sphere enters the lens's view cone (6° half-angle from the push-out camera to `LookAt`, stopping 10 short of it; `FairyRing` excepted), and puts each part's old value back on stepping back (a chopped node stays hidden). Enemies are never hidden.
- **The telescope is aimed and its zoom cleaned up (2026-10-20, the user: it didn't line up with the zoom, and parts of the tower flashed by):** the tube was 11° off in yaw and 3° in pitch from the ring; the whole telescope was turned about its `Mount` and the tube pieces (`Tube`, `Bands`, `Glass`, `Eyecup`) pitched so the eyecup → lens line runs through `LookAt` (0.4 studs off at 262), lowered 0.2 so its feet sit on the planks, and (the user: it stood by the corner post and looked into it) moved to the middle of the north rail, between the two corner posts, its mount at (101.65, 37.18, −850.8) so its line crosses the rail at the middle of the centre gap; nothing of it touches a visible tower part. The camera glides along that line. **The lookout and the player's own body are hidden locally while the lens is up:** the lens comes up, and they go, at 80% of the glide to the eyecup, and come back 20% into the glide back (`LENS_AT`). Backup `MapBackup_2026-10-20/TelescopeBefore`.
- **Secret-room exits land cleanly (2026-10-20, the user: "a touch buggy"):** `PortalService` puts the feet on the target's top face (root 3 above it), keeps the screen black `SETTLE_SECONDS` (0.15) after the move, and `FadeController` calls `AmbienceController.refresh()` as the fade-in starts, so the room's twilight never shows at the spawn. Measured: the root settles under 0.3 studs on arrival from both rooms.
- **Index world cards re-shot for the moved map (2026-10-20):** W1 `rbxassetid://85727377419936` (the old framing moved with the map), W2 `rbxassetid://114443855013343` (new for the stretched world: from above the loop over the creek's stepping stones, the pond, the paths rejoining, the lookout and the cave arch; camera (−40, 90, −705) looking at (35, 0, −845), crop rows 170..852, x from 20), W3 `rbxassetid://106928307558031`; sources `art/portraits/worlds/world_W0N_2026-10-20.png`.
- Playtested: spawn at the origin, World 1 / World 2 detected, silo → cellar (the Corn Wraith there) → spawn, ring → stump room (twilight on, the Fairy inside) → World 2's spawn, the telescope showing the ring stump with nothing in front and everything back after; clean console.

## Code: Rojo, files are the source of truth

- **Rojo 7.7.1** (Aftman): `default.project.json` maps `src/shared` → `ReplicatedStorage.Shared`, `src/server` → `ServerScriptService.Server` and `src/client` → `StarterPlayer.StarterPlayerScripts.Client`, each with `$ignoreUnknownInstances`. So Rojo never deletes Studio instances it doesn't know: a deleted file's script must also be deleted in Studio by hand.
- Run `rojo serve` in the checkout or worktree you're working in, then press Connect in Studio's Rojo plugin. **Edit the `.luau` files**; never edit scripts in Studio's editor or through `.Source` while Rojo is connected (the next sync overwrites it).
- `.gitattributes` keeps `.luau` / `.json` / `.toml` LF (`core.autocrlf` is on here, and Rojo copies bytes as-is).
- The ServerStorage dev modules (`W2Swap`, `W3Swap`, `W3RimSolver`, `DevTools/*`…) and `StarterCharacter.Animate` stay in the place file only.
- Pre-Rojo Studio backup: `CodeBackup_2026-10-17_PreRojo`, in the backups file (see Studio).
- **Studio plugin:** the only one is the local Rojo from `rojo plugin install`, kept at the CLI's version. To update: bump `aftman.toml`, run `aftman install` and `rojo plugin install`, then restart Studio and `rojo serve`. Studio's plugin manager is in the top text menu, **Plugins → Manage Plugins**.
- **Restart `rojo serve` after changing `default.project.json`;** a running server keeps the project name it started with. If it fails with "Could not start the Rojo server on 127.0.0.1:34872 … os error 10048", an old server is still running: find its PID with `netstat -ano` and stop it.

## Studio

- **The place lives on Roblox since 2026-10-23** (the user saved it: place id `101820207841975`, universe `10772560777`, owned by the user's account, private). Studio opens it from the start page (My Experiences), its title reads "Bonk Quest", and **File → Save to Roblox** saves a version (Version History; **Ctrl+Alt+S** is Save to Roblox with Notes; this Studio has no Ctrl+S on it unless the user binds one in File → Customize Shortcuts); **File → Publish** (Alt+P) is what players get, so saving never changes the live game. `place\BonkQuest.rbxlx` is now only a backup: **File → Download a Copy** over it after a big batch of work, for Google Drive. Rojo, the MCP tools and playtests work as before. Find the Studio instance with `list_roblox_studios` every session; its id changes whenever Studio reopens.
- **DataStores work in Studio playtests since 2026-10-23** (the user turned on Experience Settings → Security → Enable Studio Access to API Services; checked: 123 admin coins survived a stop and restart). So **test grants stick to the user's real save**: take back what a test gives (admin `coins` with a negative amount, `remove`, `tool { remove }`...), and wipe the user's save before launch. The game passes and products in `Config/Shop` still need real ids from the website.
- Map, UI and model work uses `execute_luau` in the **Edit** datamodel. Edit mode is unavailable while a playtest runs, so stop play first.
- Changes live in Studio's memory until the user saves. Remind them to save the place (File → Save to Roblox, or Ctrl+Alt+S) after a batch of work.
- **Backups live outside the place (2026-10-20, the user's call: the place had reached 245 MB, 70% of it old backups).** Every dated backup folder these notes name in ServerStorage (`MapBackup_*`, `*SwapBackup_*`, `DecorBackup_*`, `UIBackup_*`, `CodeBackup_2026-10-17_PreRojo`, `HouseBackup` / `FountainBackup` / `TrimsBackup` and the rest, plus `_OldFrame`) is now in `place/backups/ServerStorageBackups_2026-10-20.rbxmx`, one Folder holding all 31 (45,815 instances). To get one back, insert that file in Studio (File → Insert from file, or drag it in) and take what you need. A full copy of the place from before the cleanup is `place/backups/BonkQuest_before_backup_cleanup_2026-10-20.rbxlx`. ServerStorage now holds only `Assets`, `DevTools`, `AudioCandidates_2026-10-07` and the W2/W3 swap and offset modules. A job may still park a backup in ServerStorage, but move it out the same way once the user has approved the change.

### World folder layout (World 1 tidied 2026-10-20, World 2 the same day; W3 will follow it)

Code finds world pieces by **tag** (MapLayout), so folders are for people. The few names code and dev tools do rely on stay fixed: the world's top folders, `Ground` (enemies walk on it), `Structures/Bridges`, `Structures/Merchants`, `Spawns/EnemySpawns` / `BossSpawns` / `PlayerSpawn`, `Ground/Hub`, `BossArena`, `PathDetails`, `SecretCellar`, `Paths/Center|Left|Right`, and the ground part names `Grass`, `GrassStep`, `Step`, `Path`, `Water`, `Dirt` (Replant, PathTrims, BlockRegen classify ground by them).

```
W01_SunnyMeadows/
  Ground/        BossArena  Dirt  ElevatedGround  Hills  Hub  LowGround  PathDetails  Paths  Ramps/(Steps, GrassSteps)  Rim  River  SecretCellar
  Structures/    Bridges  Merchants  Hub/(Fountain)  Village/(House, Windmill)  Pond/(FishingHut, PondCottage, Well_East)
                 Farmstead/(Barn, Silo, Cottage, Well_West)  SecretCellar
  Decorations/   Trees/<Kind>  Bushes/<Kind>  Rocks/<Kind>  Flowers/<Colour>  GrassTufts  Logs  Fences  Pond/(LilyPads, Reeds)  Farm
  Spawns/  Enemies/  Bosses/  Gathering/  Ambience/(Lamps, Particles, Sounds)  Npcs/
```

- **Structures are grouped by area** (Hub, Village, Pond, Farmstead), each area a Folder. Bridges, Merchants and SecretCellar stay at the top.
- **Every decoration copy is named after its template** in `ServerStorage.Assets.Decorations` (`Tree_Oak_A`, `Bush_Berry_C`, `Rock_Mossy_B`, `Daisies_5`, `GrassTuft_B`, `Log_A`, `Reeds_C`, `LilyPad_B`), inside a folder for its kind (flowers by colour, since they're recoloured Tulips / Daisies). The tidy pass matched each copy to its template by its MeshIds. The fences are each their own mesh, so they keep `Fence_01`..`Fence_11`.
- **A building's parts are grouped into Models** like PondCottage and FishingHut: `Shell`, `Roof`, `Chimney`, `Front` / `Door` / `Windows`, `Tower` (Windmill), `WindowBoxes` (Cottage), with special pieces kept (`Sails`, `Details`). Groups that only sort things are Folders (`Props`, `River/Details`); a Model is for something that moves or pivots as one.
- No empty folders and no extra levels (the old `Fences/Elevated` and the empty ramp folders are gone).
- **World 2 follows the same pattern (2026-10-20, the user: a similar structure, not a copy):** `Ground/` (BossArena, CaveMouth, Creek, Grass, Hills, PathDetails, Paths, Rim, StumpRoom/Floor), `Structures/` by area (Bridges, Camp, Lookout, Merchants, Ruins, StumpRoom), `Decorations/` (Camp, FairyRing, GroundCover/<Kind>, Logs, Props (Signpost_A), Rocks/Mossy, Trees/Spruce, Vines, Water/(LilyPads, Reeds)), `Spawns`, `Npcs`, `Ambience/(Critters, Particles, Sounds)`. Both worlds keep the map base (`Rim`, `Hills`) under `Ground` (World 1's moved from `Decorations/Hedges/OuterRim` and `Decorations/Hills` the same day, so enemies now step onto low hill tiers instead of clipping through them; the probe only reaches 2.5 studs up, so never onto the rim). The dev tools `Replant` and `BlockRegen` were updated to read them there and still treat them as map base, not plain ground (`Replant` also no longer needs the moved-out `MapBackup_2026-10-18`). A `Gathering` folder comes with World 2's gathering.

## Code layout (`src/`, synced into Studio)

- `ReplicatedStorage.Shared`: `Loader`, `Remotes`, `Animation/` (see below), `Config/` (`Balance`, `Weapons`, `Enemies`, `MapLayout`, `WorldTypes`, `Worlds/W01_SunnyMeadows` + `W02_RedwoodForest` / `W03_CrystalCaverns`, `Index`, `Leveling`, `Quests`, `Shop`, `Gates`, `Gathering`, `Tools`, `Potions`, `Upgrades`, `Leaderboards`, `DailyRewards`, plus `Admin`, `Armor`, `Materials`, `Infuse`, `Fishing`, `Talkers`).
- `ServerScriptService.Server.Services`: `PlayerDataService` (saves everything a player earns; first in `SERVICE_ORDER`), `ShopService` (the Robux shop, multipliers; also hands out the timer stamps PotionService uses), `WorldEnemyService` (enemy registry, spawn, respawn), `MeleeService` (weapons + enemy AI), `AvatarService` (dresses R6 avatars), `IndexService` (the Index: kills, discoveries, weapon drops, the one saved DataStore), `CoinService` / `ExpService` (coins and EXP from kills), `QuestService` (every world's questline, run side by side: Dale, Rowan, Pip; content in `Config/Quests`), `GateService` (buying world gates; `Config/Gates`), `AdminService` (the admin panel), `GatherService` (trees and rocks broken for materials; `Config/Gathering`), `ToolService` (gathering tools owned; `Config/Tools`), `PotionService` (brewing, drinking and potion timers; `Config/Potions`), `MaterialService` (crafting materials held and dropped), `ArmorService` (armour sets), `StallService` (the Crafting and Weapons stalls), `PortalService` (teleport zones, the Worlds menu), `InfuseService` (the Infusers), `SecretService` (World 3's crystal code), `FishingService`, `HealZoneService` (the healing fountain), `UpgradeService` (the Upgrades stand; `Config/Upgrades`), `LeaderboardService` (the 9 leaderboards and their rewards; `Config/Leaderboards`), `DailyRewardService` (the Daily Rewards stand and promo codes; `Config/DailyRewards`). The order they load in is `SERVICE_ORDER` in `src/server/init.server.luau`.
- `StarterPlayer.StarterPlayerScripts.Client`: `Util/` (`Responsive`, `DamagePopup`, `HudFly`, `BonkPop`, `Format`, `WeaponTile`, `CenterRow`, `FontDrop`), `Controllers/` (`ChatTagController`, `PromptController`, `WorldEnemyController`, `EnemyAnimator`, `SprintController`, `PlayerAnimator`, `WeaponController`, `InteractionController`, `NpcIdleController`, `LootController`, `SpinnerController`, `FlowController`, `QuestController`, `GuideController`, `GatherController`, `FishingController`, `InfuserController`, `FadeController`, `TelescopeController`, `AmbienceController`, `CritterController`, `SecretCrystalController`, `HealZoneController`), `UI/` (`PanelWindow`, `RobuxShopUI`, `InventoryUI`, `PlayerCardUI`, `HotbarUI`, `ShopUI`, `IndexUI`, `IndexPopup`, `CoinsUI`, `DialogueUI`, `QuestTrackerUI`, `GateUI`, `WorldBannerUI`, `AdminUI`, `CraftingUI`, `UpgradesUI`, `DailyRewardsUI`, `StatsUI`, `LeaderboardUI`, `MaterialToastUI`, `BoostHudUI`, `InfuserUI`, `WorldsUI`, `FishingRingUI`, `PlayerListUI`, `Preloader`). The full order lists are in `src/client/init.client.luau`.
- Players start with the **Dagger** (`Weapons.default`, marked `starter = true`): always owned and can't be sold. Fists were removed.
- **One weapon in the hotbar.** `MeleeService` keeps a *selected* weapon (the player's `SelectedWeapon` attribute) that is either in hand or put away (`Holstered`). Every spawn starts with it put away. Clicking the hotbar's only slot (`WeaponHud/Hotbar/Slot1`, key 1) toggles it (`Remotes.HolsterWeapon`). Picking a weapon in the Inventory selects it and puts it in hand. The character's `Weapon` attribute is set only while the weapon is in hand. `WeaponController.current()` is the weapon in hand; `selected()` is the hotbar's. The hotbar is a frosted bubble-image slot that grows on hover; **the slot holding what's in hand (the weapon taken out, or a tool) stays grown the same way** (user, 2026-10-19; HotbarUI `refreshHeld`, the Hotbar's `HeldScale` attribute, default its `HoverScale`), with no other highlight.

  The Inventory opens from `WeaponHud/InventoryButton`, on the left edge above `IndexButton` and `ShopButton`.
  - **Side buttons, style B "Puffy toy" (2026-10-10, user's pick on https://claude.ai/artifact/RgozkjSe1u9pmQNuzJXbAd):** glossy, inflated drawn icons with a dark outline, a soft shadow and a slight tilt. Backpack `95998562954903`, book with a star `117517869868002`, market stall `83448440638931` (source `art/hud_buttons_b.html` → `art/hud_buttons/`).
  - No circle: each `Content/Background` bubble is hidden.
  - `Content/Icon` is square, 1.4 × the button's width, at the top, shifted by its art's measured off-centre (`ArtOffsetX`).
  - `Content/Label` (Luckiest Guy, white, ink `2b1b3d` stroke 3.5, max 30) has its **centre on the drawing's bottom edge**, measured per PNG (the `LabelAt` attribute on the Icon: 0.904 / 0.891 / 0.898). It has a `LabelShadow` copy just below it, and HotbarUI runs `Util.FontDrop.watch` on all three buttons.

  Roblox's own Backpack CoreGui is disabled. **Weapon icons are drawn pictures (2026-10-09, user's request: the viewports didn't pop):** each def's `icon` in `Config/Weapons` is a cel-shaded render of the weapon's mesh with an ink outline and soft shadow (Blender `plants.blend` scene `Icons`, text block `icon_render` → `art/icons/weapons/raw/`, finished by `art/weapon_icons.py` → `art/icons/weapons/<Id>.png` + `sheet.png`). Inventory tiles show it in `ItemTile/Picture` (0.96 × 0.92, behind `ItemName` and the `Stat` pill); the hotbar in `Slot1/Content/Face/Picture` (1.3× the slot, so it pokes out). Without an `icon` both fall back to the `Icon` viewport (`showWeapon`, an outline-point perspective fit with `Fill` / `Aspect` attributes). The `Stat` pill is text only and shows a weapon's DPS since 2026-10-22, "51.6 DPS" (`DpsFormat` on `Assets/UI/WeaponTile`; see "Inventory DPS"); the Damage card under the character keeps its sword. **The hotbar slot looks like the weapon's Inventory tile:** `Slot1/Content/Skin` takes the rarity image from the slot's `Skin_<Rarity>` attributes (copied from `ItemTile`); the old frosted `Background` bubble is hidden and the Face's fill/stroke off. The Hotbar sits at Y scale 0.955 (raised so the picture isn't cut off). A new weapon needs an icon rendered the same way.
- **All animation goes through `ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Animation`**, our own animation system; its root module's header maps every submodule. In short:
  - `Character.get(model)` gives one shared rig + `Animator` per character. Scripts `Play` clips on it at a `Priority` (Core < Idle < Movement < Hold < Action … < Override) instead of writing joints, so nothing fights. Additive clips layer on top.
  - Clip content lives in `Animation.Clips`: `Locomotion` (player idle/walk/sprint), `Weapons` (`HOLDS` / `SWINGS`), `Enemies` (walk, `MOVES`, flinch). Clips can carry markers (`Hit`, `Strike`, `TrailOn`…), and `Clip.fromKeyframeSequence` imports Animation Editor saves.
  - Enemies are `PartRig`s (part- or mesh-built). Only their Root is moved, and the limbs follow in one batched `BulkMoveTo`. Far or off-screen rigs update less often or not at all (`Animator.LOD`).
  - `Tween`, `Motion` (move/rotate/path/spin/bob), `Weld` (sockets; MeleeService welds weapons with `Weld.toSocket`), `Effects` (pooled bursts, shockwave, flash, trails), `CameraFX` (layered FOV zooms; it also has shake and kick, but **nothing may shake or kick the camera**: the user had every shake removed on 2026-10-09 as "a huge hassle").
  - **A delayed tween still cancels the running one at once:** playing a tween with a `DelayTime` on a property another tween is animating stops that tween straight away, not when the delay ends. Start a follow-on tween on the same property from `task.delay` (BonkPop's pop-in was cancelled this way, 2026-10-17).
  - **`Animation.Tween` reads a `Scale` goal as a model's scale** (it calls `GetScale`), so tween a UIScale with `Tween.native` (TweenService) instead (the fishing rings' pop errored this way, 2026-10-18).
  - **Every per-frame writer is an `Animation.Scheduler` system** (phases Joints / Render / Camera, ordered by priority). Never connect a new RunService handler that moves parts, joints or the camera: two connections to one event run in no guaranteed order. Profile with the `Profiling` attribute on the Scheduler module during a playtest, then read its `Stats` attribute.
- `PlayerAnimator` plays `Locomotion` on every character, and `WeaponController` plays holds, swings and the slash trail. Both expose `track(model)` / `untrack(model)` for display copies (the Inventory's character preview). `EnemyAnimator` plays enemy clips (walk, attack moves, flinch, mood faces). `SprintController` handles sprint speed (unlimited; no stamina since 2026-10-11; **no sprint at all until the Sprint upgrade**, 2026-10-19), plus the `CameraFX` "Sprint" zoom.
- Weapon ownership is server-side and saved (`MeleeService`, the `weapons` section of PlayerDataService): players start with the starter weapon only and get the rest as **enemy drops**, published as the player's `OwnedWeapons` attribute (comma-separated ids). Only owned weapons can be equipped.
  - **Drops:** each enemy's `drops` list in `Config/Worlds/<world>` (placeholder rates) is rolled per entry on every kill by `IndexService`, which calls `MeleeService.grant`.
  - **Kill hook:** `WorldEnemyService.defeat(enemyId, killer)` credits the player whose weapon hit finished the enemy, and `onDefeated` listeners hear it (Index, coins, EXP, quests).
  - **Index:** state is published as the player attributes `IndexKills` (`"Chicken=3"`) and `IndexWeapons`. Each discovery fires `Remotes.IndexDiscovered { kind, id }` for the NEW popup. `Shared.Config.Index` holds the roster, drop and source lookups. The Inventory window (`StarterGui/Inventory`) lists everything owned (weapons, tools, armour, potions, materials), with a filter dropdown, search, Equip Best and Sell Mode.
  - **Selling (2026-10-20, the user: every weapon, tool, armour set, potion and material sells, priced per item):** each def in `Config/Weapons` / `Tools` / `Armor` / `Potions` (its `SELL` table) / `Materials` has a `sell` price (random placeholders scaled by rarity and world, a weapon at most 40% of its stall price; the user will tune them). `Shared.Config.Sell.price(kind, id)` is the one rule: never the starter, `keep` items (Rusty Blade, Worldheart Blades), quest items or cores. `Server.Services.SellService` answers `Remotes.SellItems` (a RemoteFunction: `{ { kind, id, count? } } -> ok, coins`), checks each line against what is held (stacks capped at the count held, each line once), takes the items through their own services (`MeleeService.removeWeapons`, which replaced `MeleeService.SellItems`; `ToolService.revoke`; `ArmorService.revoke`; `PotionService.add` / `MaterialService.add` negative) and pays through `CoinService.add(..., "sell")`, which the leaderboards don't count as earned. Selling the selected weapon selects the Dagger; selling the worn set takes it off. **In Sell Mode** every tile's pill shows its price (the WeaponTile's `SellFormat` "%s Coins", `NoSellText` "Can't sell"); a weapon, tool or set toggles its pick, and a potion or material opens the tile's hand-built `SellMenu` (a DrinkMenu copy: `Title` "How many?", `Cancel` ×, `Minus`, `Count` TextBox, `Plus`, `Worth` (`WorthFormat`), `All` gold, `Pick` green) from 1 to all held, then the pill reads "99 / 100" (`PickedFormat`). **Picking a Legendary or rarer weapon, tool or set asks first** (the user, 2026-10-20): the tile's hand-built `ConfirmMenu` (a DrinkMenu copy: `Title` "Are you sure?", `Worth` (`WorthFormat`), `Yes` green, `Cancel` red); Yes picks it. **A sold weapon can drop again** (the user: a coin farm is fine). **Selling stays out of the World 1 timing.** The Inventory header's `Capacity` pill now just counts the different things held (`Format` "%s Items"; the "35 / 50" bag size and its `Max` are gone). `BottomBar/SellWorth` (the Weaponsmith's coin pill, `Format` "+%s") shows the picks' total next to `Sell (N)`, and the Hint reads `Text_Sell`. Playtested: 345 coins for a mixed sale with refused lines, then 8,643 through the UI (a weapon and 99 Stone), clean console; the confirm opened for the Weathervane Spear and Rock Candy Club (Yes picked, Cancel didn't) and not for the Scythe (Epic).
- Modules use the Loader `init(deps)` / `start()` pattern. A **new module must be added to the order list** in `ServerScriptService.Server` (`SERVICE_ORDER`) or the `Client` LocalScript (`UTIL_ORDER` / `CONTROLLER_ORDER` / `UI_ORDER`).
- New RemoteEvents are declared in `Shared.Remotes` (`EVENT_NAMES`).
- Style: `--!strict`, explanatory block comments at the top of each module and above non-obvious code, names that match the surrounding code.

## Game rules that are easy to break

- **Weapons are the only combat.** Hitting an enemy starts a real-time fight (`MeleeService`). The dice duel ("Challenge" prompt, `CombatService` / `CombatEngine`, `CombatUI`, `BattleScene` and its `BattleArena`, `BattleNumber`, the duel moves attack / heal / shield / nothing / hit, `Types`, `Balance.Dice` / `HandMultipliers` / `Player` / `Enemy`, enemy `attack` / `heal` / `shield` / `actions`, `EngagedBy`, `LogicTests`, `CombatSim`) was **scratched and deleted on 2026-10-08** at the user's request. Don't bring any of it back. Enemy max HP is just `hp` in `Config/Worlds` (the boss's 32 × 4 became 128).
- **Players are locked to R6** via `StarterPlayer.StarterCharacter` (an R6 rig) plus `AvatarService`, which re-applies each player's outfit. Keep both; the place is unpublished, so the Game Settings avatar option can't be used yet.
- Gear, talents and chests were removed on purpose. Don't reintroduce them unless asked. (Leaderboards were too, until the user asked for them back on 2026-10-19: see "Leaderboards".)
- **Everything a player earns is saved (2026-10-09, user's request: "everything the player can obtain").** `PlayerDataService`: DataStore `PlayerData`, key `u_<UserId>`, one record `{ v = 1, [section] = value }`.
  - Each service registers its section in `init()` with `PlayerDataService.register(name, { load = fn(player, value), save = fn(player) -> value })`. Sections: `highestWorld` (own, the `HighestWorld` attribute, raised by buying world gates; `unlockWorld(player, n)`), `weapons` (owned + selected), `coins`, `exp` (level follows), `quest` (step/state/progress per line), `shop` (passes, boost seconds left, starter pack, the last 50 receipt ids), `materials`, `armor`, `tools`, `infuse` and `potions`.
  - Loaders run in registration order (= `SERVICE_ORDER`), so coins/EXP are in place before the quest loads and never count as progress. A loader can run before the service's own PlayerAdded handler, so it must create state itself. **Savers read the published attributes** (or state kept a while after leaving), because PlayerRemoving handlers run in no set order.
  - Saved every 60 s, on leave, on shutdown, and by `saveNow()` for purchase receipts. A record that failed to load is never written over. Players get `DataLoaded` = true once applied.
  - DataStores only work once the place is published with Studio API access on; until then it warns once and progress lasts the session (as before). The Index still has its own store (`IndexService`).
- **Coins came back on 2026-10-07 (user's request); saved since 2026-10-09.** Kill coins × the player's `CoinBoost`.
  - Each kill gives a random amount from the enemy's `coins` range in `Config/Worlds`, via `CoinService`, published as the player's `Coins` attribute.
  - `Remotes.CoinsDropped` plays the spill: `LootController` flings 3–5 copies of `ReplicatedStorage/Assets/Effects/Coin`, which sit spinning for a second, then become `WeaponHud/Templates/FlyingCoin`s that fly to the counter (`Util.HudFly`).
  - `CoinsUI` counts the counter up over the `Coins` frame's `CountSeconds`, starting when the first coin lands.
  - The spill is announced before the balance changes, so the counter knows to wait.
- **EXP and levels (2026-10-07; saved since 2026-10-09):** kill EXP × `ExpBoost`.
  - Each kill adds the enemy's `exp` (`Config/Worlds`) to the player's `ExpTotal` attribute, via `ExpService`.
  - `Config/Leveling` turns the total into level, EXP into that level, and EXP needed. Players start at **level 1** (`Leveling.START`, user's request 2026-10-08; the `LevelBadge` placeholder reads 1). **The curve (2026-10-19, the user: levelling much quicker, because early HP is scarce): 30 to reach level 2, then +15 per level** (it was 100, +50), and **max HP is 100 + 10 per level** (the user's call; it was +15 for a day). Every level requirement moved to the level that needs the same total EXP, so it still comes at the same point in a world: armour 3 → 5, 8 → 14, 11 → 20, 15 → 27, 19 → 35, 23 → 42; quest level steps 5 → 9, 8 → 14, 12 → 22, 15 → 27, 20 → 36, 23 → 42 (dialogue too); the World 2 gate 10 → 18, then **25** (the user's pick, 2026-10-19), World 3 20 → 36. Armour health rose with it so a set adds about a quarter of your HP at its level (first World 1 set) up to about half (World 3): Farmhand's Gear 20 → 35, Harvest Knight 40 → 75, Woodland Hide 60 → 110, Heartwood Bark 90 → 160, Cave Runner 120 → 220, Crystal Queen 160 → 280. **World 2 and 3 enemies now hit relatively softer** (players have about 1.4–1.6× the HP there); tune them when those worlds are balanced with the simulation. **A level up heals to full** (`ExpService`, only on a rise). `Level` is published too.
  - `Remotes.ExpDropped` spills 3–5 blue `Assets/Effects/ExpStar`s that fly to the `XP` bar as `FlyingStar`s.
  - `PlayerCardUI` counts the bar ("EXP", "x / y") and the `LevelBadge` up over a second.
- **No stamina (user, 2026-10-11):** sprinting is unlimited once unlocked: there's no base sprint since 2026-10-19, the Sprint upgrade unlocks it (`Config/Upgrades`; `Balance.Sprint` is gone). The card's `Stamina` bar was deleted and `XP` moved up into its place.
- **The coin icon everywhere is the coin stack** (`rbxassetid://114082700684383`, the Coins upgrade's set A icon; user, 2026-10-19). It replaced the old single coin `87196228547336` on all 42 live UI pieces (the HUD counter and `FlyingCoin`, every shop and Buy button, cost pills, the Index slip, the quest card's `CoinIcon` and the tracker's, gate signs, the Robux shop balance, the admin panel, the leaderboard's Coins tab). Old backups in ServerStorage keep the old one. A new coin image anywhere uses the stack.
- **Numbers read with commas everywhere (2026-10-11):** `Client.Util.Format` (`commas`, `ratio`) for coins, EXP, the level badge, health, enemy health bars, quest counts and reward chips, like the shop. **Coins shorten from a million (2026-10-19, the user):** `Format.coins` shows every coin amount (the HUD counter, shop and stall prices, balances, rewards, gate signs, the Robux shop's bundles, the Index's costs, the admin panel, the Coins leaderboard and the player list) in full up to 999,999, then rounded down to one decimal: 1.1M, 1.5B, 2T (a ".0" is dropped). A new coin label uses `Format.coins`, never `commas`.
- **World banner (2026-10-11):** `Client.UI.WorldBannerUI` + hand-built `StarterGui/WorldBanner` (`Banner` CanvasGroup with `PopScale`, `Subtitle` "World 2", `Title` + `TitleShadow` = the world's `name`; `InSeconds` / `HoldSeconds` / `OutSeconds` / `PopFrom`). Shows on join and whenever the player walks into another world. A world's footprint is the X/Z box of its `Ground` parts (parts over 400 studs, World 1's Baseplate, ignored), smallest first.
- **Defeat burst (2026-10-11, user: kills were too quiet):** `WorldEnemyController.showDefeat`: `Poof` + new `DeathPop` stars (EffectPresets) + a `Shockwave` ring, sized by the body, and `ReplicatedStorage/Assets/Sounds/EnemyPop` (Creator Store "Cartoon Pop - Daily Sounds", `106984966606682`). Not for bodies already dead on join (`ready`). **BONK! pop (2026-10-17):** `Client.Util.BonkPop` clones the hand-built `ReplicatedStorage/Assets/UI/BonkPop` (BillboardGui: `Content` with `PopScale`, `Rays` (the popup rays image, orange, 2.3×) and `RaysInner` (yellow, 1.6×, spinning the other way), `Shadow`, `Label`, Luckiest Guy white with ink stroke) over the body's top: it slams in with an overshoot at a random tilt (`TiltMax`), holds, then rises, shrinks and fades. Attributes `Words` (comma list), `Lifetime`, `PopSeconds`, `PopFrom`, `Hold`, `Rise`, `Height`, `RaysSpin`, `ShrinkTo`, `SizeMin` / `SizeMax`, `BossScale`.
- **Hit bubble, hit sound, footsteps, owl and campfire (2026-10-21, the user's requests):** every weapon hit on an enemy shows its damage in a comic burst bubble that pops like the BONK (`Util.BonkPop.hit(position, size, amount, isBoss)`, called from `WorldEnemyController.showHit` in place of the old `DamagePopup` "Enemy" number; `DamagePopup` still does the player, heal and info popups). Template `ReplicatedStorage/Assets/UI/HitPop` (BillboardGui 130 x 100: `Content` with `PopScale`, red `Rays` (BonkPop's rays image, e8402e), the white ink-outlined `Burst` (`rbxassetid://134101757868745`, `art/tools/ui/hit_burst.py` -> `svg_render.py` -> `art/ui/hud/hit_burst.png`), `Shadow` / `Label` (Luckiest Guy, red ff4b3a, ink stroke 3)); BonkPop's motion attributes plus `Format` ("%s") and `Spread` (1.2 studs off-centre at random so quick hits don't stack). Every image in a pop's `Content` fades; only `Rays*` turn. A hit also plays `Assets/Sounds/EnemyHit` (pitch ×0.92-1.10 on its own speed). **Footsteps follow the legs:** `Animation.Clips.Locomotion` calls `params.onStep(foot, sprint)` as the stride phase crosses π/2 and 3π/2 (where the bob is lowest; only grounded and moving), and `PlayerAnimator` plays `Assets/Sounds/Footstep` (attributes `PitchJitter` 0.08, `SprintBoost` 0.3) from two copies on the root; Roblox's looping `Running` sound is muted. Measured: one step every 4.5 studs, 0.19 s apart at walk speed. **The owl hoots** (`Owl/Root/Hoot`, positional, `CallMin` / `CallMax` 8 / 16 s): `CritterController` plays it with a small puff while the camera is within its RollOffMaxDistance (80). **The campfire crackles:** a looped `Crackle` in `W02…/Decorations/Camp/Campfire_A/Embers` (InverseTapered 6..35, Ambience group). The user's picks (from the audition folder `SoundService/AudioCandidates_2026-10-21`): hit G, PSE "Beefy cartoon punch" `9117970227`; owl C, PSE echoey hoots `9117181540`; fire B, "Quiet campfire" `98708119672006`; footstep K, a community "cartoon bubble pop" `109359226723492` at speed 0.8 (picked from six candidates matched to the death pop), with `PlaybackRegion` 0.12.. because the file starts with 0.12 s of silence that put every step late (found by sampling `PlaybackLoudness` per frame in a playtest). **The bubble and the BONK are only for the player who landed the hit** (2026-10-23, the user: other players saw them): `WorldEnemyService.damage` sets the enemy's `HitBy` attribute (the attacker's UserId) before `Hp`, and `WorldEnemyController` draws the bubble (and, on the killing hit, the BONK) only when `HitBy` is the local player; everyone still sees the spark, flash, recoil and death cloud and hears the hit and the pop. **A death shows only the BONK and its pop** (the user, 2026-10-21/22): `showHit` skips both the `EnemyHit` sound and the hit bubble when the hit leaves 0 HP. **The bubble always draws over the health bar:** billboards drawn on top are sorted by distance from the camera, and the bubble sat level with the bar with a random 1.2-stud nudge in any direction, so about half drew behind it; now `BonkPop` spreads a pop sideways on screen only and pulls it the template's `Pull` studs (3, on `HitPop` and `BonkPop`) toward the camera. Measured: every bubble 2.8-3.1 studs nearer than the bar.
- **Player hit, low health and healing feedback (2026-10-22, the user's requests):** `Client.Controllers.HurtController`. **A hit:** when an enemy's attack lands (`MeleeService`, after `TakeDamage`) the server fires `Remotes.PlayerHurt { character, from }` to every client: the body glows red (`EffectPresets.PlayerHurt`, a Highlight fill ff2828 at 0.3 fading over 0.45 s, the same as `WeaponHud/HitFlash`'s red screen flash) and recoils (`Animation.Clips.Hurt.flinch`, additive at Override like an enemy's flinch: torso back 14°, head 16°, arms forward 24° / out 10°, snapping in over 0.07 s, gone by 0.42 s); the hit player alone **stumbles back**, away from the enemy: a `HurtPush` LinearVelocity on the root (ground axes only, 36 studs/s easing to 0 over 0.3 s) plus an 8 studs/s hop, never while `InputLocked`. A plain velocity moved the body only 0.4 studs (Roblox's walking cancels it); measured with the push: 3.5-3.7 studs straight back, up 0.14, torso 12-13° back. InventoryUI still flashes the screen and pops the damage number. **Low health:** `StarterGui/ScreenGlow` (IgnoreGuiInset, DisplayOrder -1, so it covers the top bar and draws under every other gui) holds `LowHealth`, a white edge-glow image (`rbxassetid://107098356725444`, `art/tools/ui/hurt_fx.py` -> `art/ui/hud/edge_glow.png`: clear middle, the glow from 68% out) tinted from `ColorLight` ff3b30 to `ColorDark` 8a0000 and from `MinStrength` 0.3 to `MaxStrength` 0.92 as health falls from `StartBelow` (0.5) to 0, with a heartbeat that quickens and deepens (`PulseRate` 1.1, `PulseDepth` 0.25), easing over `EaseSeconds` (a Scheduler Render system, `LowHealthGlow`). Measured: none at 60%, 0.4 at 42 HP, 0.61-0.75 and dark red at 15. **A healing potion** (InventoryUI, a drunk `Health` potion -> `HurtController.healed()`): green + signs float off the body (`EffectPresets.HealPlus`, `rbxassetid://90672393638076`, `art/ui/hud/heal_plus.png`) and `ScreenGlow/HealGlow` (the same edge image in green 4fcf5c) comes in over `InSeconds` 0.12 to `Strength` 0.6, holds `HoldSeconds` 0.25 and fades over `OutSeconds` 0.6 (measured: peak 0.60, about a second).
- **Inventory DPS, the stat cards and armour regen / speed (2026-10-22, the user: Equip Best picked slow secret weapons over the Worldheart Blade; option B on https://claude.ai/artifact/6LM4U8XgrVihfD1RrWvCMQ with both cards on the left).** Weapons are compared by damage per second: `WeaponTile.dps(def)` (damage / cooldown after `Infuse.adjust`, so the Worldheart tiers count), `dpsNumber` (one decimal below 100, commas above) and `dpsText` (the tile's `DpsFormat`); every weapon tile's pill reads "393 DPS", and InventoryUI sorts and picks Equip Best by it (measured: Worldheart Blade III 393 over the Rock Candy Club 357). **The Inventory `Window` is smaller** (Size 0.70 x 0.74 scale with its 1.71 aspect, centre x 0.58, so it and the cards sit centred together; the old window is in `ServerStorage/MapBackup_2026-10-22/InventoryWindowBefore`) and holds two hand-built cards hanging off its left edge (anchor (1, 0) at x -0.02, 0.21 of its width): **`WeaponStats`** (`Head` red e8574a with a `Content` CenterRow of `IconRing/Icon` (the Attack sword) + `Title` "Weapon"; `ItemName`; `Dps/Value` gold and `Dps/Unit` "DPS"; `Row_Damage` / `Row_Range` / `Row_Speed` "Attack speed", each `Label` + `Value`; attributes `RangeFormat` "%s", `SpeedFormat` "%s s") and **`ArmorStats`** (blue 3f8ae8 head with the Defense shield; `ItemName` / `EmptyName` "No armor"; `Row_Health` / `Row_Defense` / `Row_Regen` / `Row_Speed`; `NoneText` "None"). Each card is a `Face` (white, 8fd9e6 rim) over a `Lip`, with its own `Blocker`; `On*` / `Off*` `Fill` / `Label` / `Value` colour attributes grey out a bonus the set lacks. InventoryUI's `renderStatCards` fills them on every render and runs `FontDrop.watch` on both. The Damage / Health / Defense mini cards under the character stay. **Armour regen and speed** (`Config/Armor` `regen?` / `speed?`, the user's approved table: World 1 none, Woodland Hide +5% speed, Heartwood Bark +0.25%/s regen, Cave Runner +10% speed, Crystal Queen +0.5%/s and +8%; Worlds 4-7 both, up to about +2%/s and +25%): `ArmorService` publishes `ArmorRegen` / `ArmorSpeed`, UpgradeService's regen tick adds `ArmorRegen`, and `SprintController` multiplies walk and sprint by Swiftness x (1 + ArmorSpeed), capped at `1 + Armor.MAX_SPEED` (0.5) together. StatsUI shows an "Armour +x%" chip on Regen and on Sprint (even while Sprint is locked). Playtested: Crystal Queen walks 25.92 (24 x 1.08), Woodland Hide 25.20, Heartwood Bark 24; Heartwood regen measured 1.25% of max HP a second (1.01% with its share off); the cards follow weapon and armour changes; clean console. **A bonus shows only where the set has it (2026-10-22, the user), and every weapon shows four stats:** the Inventory's `ArmorStats` hides `Row_Regen` / `Row_Speed` for a set without them (Speed moves up into Regen's slot by each row's `SlotY`; the card's `Face` / `Lip` / `Blocker` end under the last row shown); the Crafter's set cards sit in `Info/Cards` (a horizontal UIListLayout, cards 0.235 wide, LayoutOrder Defense, Health, Regen, Speed, Effect, Lasts) with new `RegenCard` (green, the Regen icon) and `SpeedCard` (red, the Sprint sneaker) shown only for a set with the bonus; the Index armour slip's `ArmorStats` has a second row `RegenStat` / `SpeedStat` (`RegenFormat` / `SpeedFormat`), packed left and shown only when the set has them, and the weapon slip's `WeaponStats` is `DpsStat`, `DamageStat` / `ReachStat`, `SpeedStat` (seconds a swing, `SpeedFormat` "%s s Speed") after `Infuse.adjust`; when a second row shows, IndexUI's `lowerForStats` moves `DropsTitle`, `RowsClip` (shorter), `ArmorCost` and `WeaponCost` down by the stats frame's `ExtraRow` (0.0769 of the slip; the cost rows keep their spot in an `ArmorPosition` attribute). The Weaponsmith's weapon rows have a purple `Dps` chip in front of Damage / Speed / Reach (`WeaponTile.dpsText`). Backups `ServerStorage/MapBackup_2026-10-22/CrafterInfoBefore`, `IndexDropSlipBefore`, `WeaponsShopListBefore`. Playtested: Woodland Hide shows Speed only and Heartwood Bark Regen only in all three places, Crystal Queen both, World 1 sets neither; the Geode Glaive's slip reads 177 DPS / 195 Damage / 11.5 Reach / 1.1 s; the Trowel's row "15 DPS"; potion cards unchanged; clean console. `world_sim.py` counts armour speed and regen since 2026-10-23 (re-run that day on current configs: World 1 30.8 min to the gate, 0.08 deaths; World 2 48.0 min prepared, 1.1 deaths, 53.2 unprepared, 4.8 deaths; players reach World 3 at level 50 with both World 2 sets and the Firefly Lantern, about 320 coins spare).
- **Every stat icon is the Upgrades panel's (2026-10-22, the user: "consistent everywhere"):** the Inventory's Damage / Health / Defense mini cards, the Crafter's set cards, the Upgrades panel's Now / Next cards, the Index slip (and its Weapons / Armour tabs and headers), the Weaponsmith's stat chips, the EXP star spots (`FlyingStar`, the Index slip, the quest reward chip, the admin EXP card) all use the set-A icons from `Config/Upgrades` (old ids and the swap are in the `roblox-ui-icons` skill). Two new set-A icons from `art/tools/icons/range_icons.py`: **Range** (an arrow in a bullseye, the arrow shorter than the target) `rbxassetid://84963496941015` on every reach stat, and **DPS** (the sword with a bolt) `rbxassetid://127525253284060` on the Index slip's `DpsStat` and the Weaponsmith's `Dps` chip (it now has a `Glyph` like the others; every chip glyph is 0.86 of the chip). **The Inventory's two stat cards carry an icon on every row too** (`Row_*/Icon`, the row's Upgrades icon left of its `Label`; it fades to the card's `OffIconTransparency` 0.55 on a greyed row). Checked in a playtest.
- **"Armor", never "Armour", in anything a player reads (the user, 2026-10-22):** every label, attribute and config string says Armor (the Inventory card, the Index tab and header, the Stats chips "Armor +5%", the admin message, Dale's and Pip's lines, two weapon blurbs). Code identifiers and comments may still say armour.
- **The Inventory's equipment slots show the item's tile at the slot's full size (2026-10-22, the user):** `Equipment/<Weapon|Armor>/Inner/RarityTile` is 1 / 0.86 of `Inner` (so it reaches the slot's edge, as big as the empty placeholder) with `Picture` at an Inventory tile's 0.96 x 0.92 of it; `renderSockets` hides the slot's dashed `Skin` while something is in it. A weapon is always selected (the Dagger at least), so only the Armor slot ever shows its placeholder; the Edit view shows the Dagger's tile.
- **The Index's eight tabs are laid out on the pill's face (2026-10-22, the user: "all of them are off", then: a taller row, bigger counts, the words centred between the icon and the tab's right edge):** `RightPage/Tabs` is 0.215 of the page tall (was 0.16; `Grid` starts at 0.425 and is shorter by as much). Each `Tab<Section>` has its `Icon` square at 0.58 of the pill's height, 0.065 in from the left and centred on the face (source rows 8..100 of 136), and `Label` (Luckiest Guy) over `Count` (FredokaOne) centred as a pair on the face (name 0.33, gap 0.04, count 0.30 of it) centred left-right at **0.61 of the tab's width** (boxes 0.559 wide, so TREASURES still fits by height and every name draws the same size: 18 / 16 px at a 1153-px-tall screen). The exact midpoint between the icon's right edge and the tab's right edge (0.658) still read as off to the right (the user, 2026-10-22), because the pill's round right end eats into the face; 0.61 looks centred. Their old UIPaddings had both sides positive (squeezing, never dropping the letters); IndexUI now runs `FontDrop.watch` on `Tabs`. Vertically every name / count pair sits on the face's middle within 0.1 px. Backup `ServerStorage/MapBackup_2026-10-22/IndexTabsBefore`.
- **Critical hits (2026-10-21, the user: 1.5x damage at 10%):** `Balance.Melee.critChance` 0.10 / `critMultiplier` 1.5, rolled per target in `MeleeService.WeaponHit`. `WorldEnemyService.damage(id, amount, attacker, crit)` sets the enemy's `CritHp` attribute to the HP a crit left (before `Hp`, so the client reads both together; any other hit clears it), and `WorldEnemyController` draws that hit's bubble as a crit: `HitPop`'s `CritScale` (1.3), `CritBurstColor` (gold ffd23f) and `CritRaysColor` (orange ff8a2b), and the hand-built `CritTag` ("CRIT!", Luckiest Guy white with ink stroke, over the burst's top, hidden otherwise); `EnemyHit` plays lower and louder (`CritPitch` 0.85, `CritVolume` 1.4). `world_sim.py` rolls crits too (`PLAYER.critChance` / `critMultiplier`): World 1 stays 30.9 min to the gate (level-bound), World 2 49.5 -> 48.1. Playtested: a Dagger hits a Bull for 5, a crit 8 with the gold bubble; a chicken's last hit showed its bubble and the BONK with only the pop; clean console. **A crit killing blow pops the CRITICAL BONK (2026-10-24, the user's pick: option B on https://claude.ai/artifact/3pu9vxxKCTX5dGo8mZaeGA):** `WorldEnemyController.showDefeat` reads the enemy's `CritHp` (0 when the kill was a crit) and passes `crit` to `BonkPop.show`, which copies the hand-built `ReplicatedStorage/Assets/UI/CritBonkPop` instead (a BonkPop copy, 250 x 165: a gold `Label` / `Shadow` BONK!, a red `Critical` / `CriticalShadow` line above it from its `CritWords` "CRITICAL", gold `RaysInner`, white `Sparkles` (the Index popup's sparkles); Lifetime 1.25, Hold 0.55, TiltMax 8), and `Assets/Sounds/EnemyPop` plays lower and louder (`CritPitch` 0.85, `CritVolume` 1.4, like EnemyHit's). Only for the player who landed it, like the BONK. Playtested (a chicken's attributes set on the client): a normal kill popped BonkPop, a crit kill CritBonkPop; clean console.
- **UI and reward sounds, and the level-up pop (2026-10-22, the user's picks from `SoundService/AudioCandidates_2026-10-22`, 13 categories A..L).** `Client.Util.UiSound.play(name)` plays a copy of the Sound `ReplicatedStorage/Assets/Sounds/UI/<name>` (SoundService `UI` SoundGroup, volume 0.8; not positional). Each Sound's `Pick` attribute names its candidate; its attributes `MinGap` (default 0.05), `PitchJitter`, `LadderStep` / `LadderMax` / `LadderReset` (each play within LadderReset seconds climbs a step in pitch, so a spill rises like a tune) and `Attack` / `Release` (a volume envelope) tune it. Where they play: `Hover` / `Click` on every clickable button and `Refuse` on a locked one (`Util.ButtonFx`; `FxNoSound` opts a button out), `PanelOpen` / `PanelClose` (`PanelWindow`), `CoinLand` / `ExpLand` as each coin or star lands (CoinsUI, PlayerCardUI), `Purchase` (stall, Upgrades, Crafter brew and craft, a sale, a gate opening) or `Refuse` when the server says no (also a refused drink, code, Infuser), `Drink`, `QuestAccept` / `QuestComplete` (QuestController; Daily claim and codes use QuestComplete), `NewItem` (IndexPopup), `LevelUp`. The picks: Hover Daily Sounds "Tiny Click" `106623224876186`; Click a community "ui-simple-button-click" `88442833509532`; PanelOpen and PanelClose Daily Sounds "Fast Whoosh" `80984744279155`, both played the same way (the user: the whole whoosh peaked 0.6 s in, long after the panel had popped open; a true reverse for the close isn't possible, a negative PlaybackSpeed clamps to 0 on both Sound and AudioPlayer): its loud middle (PlaybackRegion 0.3..0.92) with `Attack` 0.06 / `Release` 0.45, loud from 0.05 s, peak at 0.2 s, gone by 0.5 s (open at speed 1.1, close at 1); CoinLand Daily Sounds "Retro Coin Collect" `138309872562566` (ladder 0.06); ExpLand community "Twinkle08" `135385970610304`, trimmed to 0.72..2.0 because its sparkle is 0.8 s in (ladder 0.04); Purchase the Retro Coin Collect at 0.8; Refuse Daily Sounds "Error Buzz" `91501650514390`; QuestAccept community xylophone two-note climb `82488561018614`; QuestComplete APM "In Toytown (a)" `1841173364`; NewItem community "bling_diamond_pickup_2" `4612374393`; LevelUp community "Cute level up" `105193542038988`; Drink PSE comic wet pop `9113856861`. **Check every new sound for a silent lead-in** (sample `PlaybackLoudness` per frame in a playtest) and trim it with `PlaybackRegion`: the chop / mine hit `Assets/Sounds/SecretThud` (PSE `9118654831`) started 0.38 s late, "half a second" with its lower random pitches (the user); now region 0.37..1.27, loud on the first frame. The game is going a retro way with its sounds (the user, 2026-10-22); retro music for Worlds 1 and 2 is being auditioned from `SoundService/MusicCandidates_2026-10-22` (17 APM "Retro Gaming" / DistroKid chiptune tracks, A..Q; both are licensed). Daily Sounds (`DailySoundsFX`, the enemy death pop's pack: 60 sounds synthesized from scratch, search "<Name> - Daily Sounds") is the first place to look for a new UI sound. **Level up, BONK-style over your own head, never a top-of-screen banner (the user):** `Util.BonkPop.levelUp(head, level)` clones the hand-built `ReplicatedStorage/Assets/UI/LevelPop` (a BonkPop copy, 200 x 130: gold `Rays` / pale `RaysInner`, `Label` / `Shadow` "LEVEL UP!" from its `Words`, a gold `Level` / `LevelShadow` line from `LevelFormat` "Level %s"; Lifetime 1.7, Hold 0.85, Height 2.4, TiltMax 5) on an attachment in the Head, so it rides with the player; `BonkPop.play` now takes an options table (`isBoss`, `crit`, `follow`, `texts`). PlayerCardUI fires it when the badge reaches a level the server raised after `DataLoaded` (the join's count up from 0 stays quiet); a count passing two levels within `LEVEL_MERGE` (0.9 s) raises the number in the same pop. Measured: the pop on the head 1.1 s after an admin level-up (as the bar ticks over), "LEVEL UP!" 211 px wide on a 2256-px-wide screen; a chicken kill played 5 rising CoinLands and 4 ExpLands 0.04-0.1 s apart; clean console.
- **Gathering (2026-10-18, first pass; plan in the `gathering-plan` memory):** trees and rocks are broken with **tools**, never weapons.
  - A node is a model tagged `GatherNode` in its world's `Gathering` folder; its `Node` attribute names a `Config/Gathering` def (`name`, `world`, `verb`, `tool` kind, lowest `tier`, `hp`, `respawn`, `drops`). `GatherService` gives each a `NodeId` on start.
  - **Tools** (`Config/Tools`, owned and saved by `ToolService`, published as `OwnedTools`): kinds `Axe` / `Pickaxe` / `Shovel` / `Rod`, one tier per world (`tier`, `power`, `price`, plus a weapon's swing fields: `handed`, `cooldown`, `hitTime`, `reach`, `arc`, `maxTargets`, `swing`). Models are in `ReplicatedStorage/Assets/Tools/<id>` (a `Grip` PrimaryPart like a weapon). World 1: `WoodenAxe` and `WoodenPickaxe` (power 10; Dagger hold, `Diagonal` / `Mine` swings). **Their models are Blender meshes since 2026-10-18** (`w1_tools_build` + the face pass `w1_tools_clean`, `art/meshes/w1_tools.fbx` + offsets, placeholders in `ServerStorage/ToolBackup_2026-10-18`): the axe has a carved pale-wood bearded head, twine lashing and a barn-red grip; the pickaxe a curved wooden pick with flint points and a meadow-green grip; and the `WoodenRod` (jointed bamboo, black grip, blue reel, line guides; see Fishing). Each mesh is welded to the invisible `Grip`; a mesh grip is named `GripWrap`. **Icons (2026-10-19)**, rendered like the weapons' (`w1_icons_rerender` `render(id)`, the rod's thin bamboo and guides without ink; finished by `art/tools/ui/tool_icons.py` → `art/icons/tools/`): axe `rbxassetid://120793441722751`, pickaxe `123876463543492`, rod `91808020801922` (`icon` on the defs; the hotbar slots show them).
  - **Hotbar:** `Hotbar/UIListLayout.Padding` is 0.028 (user, 2026-10-19: the slots were too close together; was 0.0066). `Slot2`..`Slot5` are copies of `Slot1` with a `ToolKind` attribute (keys 2–5, `Tools.Kinds` order). A slot shows only once a tool of its kind is owned and holds the best (highest tier) one. **One thing in hand:** `MeleeService.holdTool` (`Remotes.HoldTool { kind }`) puts the weapon away; taking the weapon out puts the tool away. The character's `Tool` attribute names the tool in hand (`Weapon` unset) and the player's `HeldTool` its kind. A tool swing is rate-limited and mirrored through `SwingSeq` like a weapon's but never sends `WeaponHit`.
  - `Client.Controllers.GatherController` hooks `WeaponController.onSwingLanded` (the enemies' reach-and-arc test): a node the tool can break wobbles, puffs `Dust`, plays `SecretThud` and goes in `Remotes.GatherHit { ids }`; a wrong kind or too-low tier shows a "Needs a Pickaxe" / "Needs a Stone Axe" `DamagePopup`. `GatherService` re-checks the owned tool in hand, its kind and tier, the cooldown and the reach, then takes `power` off.
  - **Per player:** each player has their own hp and respawn timer per node. A broken node vanishes only for that player (`LocalTransparencyModifier`, local CanCollide off; an optional `Remains` child shows instead) and fades back after `respawn`. Damage heals after `Gathering.healAfter` (12 s) without a hit. Not saved.
  - The health bar is a clone of `Assets/UI/EnemyHealthBar` (`NodeBarSeconds` attribute, default 4). Drops go through `MaterialService.drop` (saved, side toast). Gathered materials have `gathered = true` and `source` = the node id (`Index.materialSource` reads it).
  - World 1: `OakTree` (Axe 1, 50 hp, 2–3 `OakLog`) and `Boulder` (Pickaxe 1, 60 hp, 2–3 `Stone`), both 45 s respawn.
  - **Node models (2026-10-18/19, Blender meshes):** templates `ServerStorage/Assets/Gathering/OakTree` and `Boulder` (tag `GatherNode`, `Node` attribute, pivot at the ground centre), each with its `Remains` model inside. Blender `w1_gather_build` + face pass `w1_gather_clean` (collection `W1_Gathering`) → `art/meshes/w1_gathering.fbx` + offsets. They must read as different from the decorations (the gathering plan): the **oak** has a boxy bevelled trunk with a pale `Fresh` axe notch on its front (−Z), four root flares, and a two-green canopy of overlapping bevelled boxes (`Leaves` / `LeavesLight`, walk-through); its `Remains` is a bevelled stump (`Trunk`, pale `Fresh` top, flat `Ring` frames, roots). **Skill pass 2026-10-19 (user: run them through the skill, same look):** the old `w1_gather_clean` face pass had crossed a trunk face (a hole on the back-right corner) and cut open seams in the canopy, so both were rebuilt from `w1_gather_build` (the trunk now in levels with its corner bevel in the outline, because Blender's bevel broke at that corner), the pale notch, stump top and rings are flat faces lying 0.004 on the wood instead of half-sunk slabs, then `drop_hidden_faces` (bottoms in the ground) and `ngonify`; 0 self-crossings, 0 loose edges, open edges only where hidden (bottoms, the trunk top inside the canopy). **No chips on the ground** (user: they looked like hit effects). Solid: `Trunk` (Hull), `Rock` (PreciseConvexDecomposition). **Placed 2026-10-19 (user: scattered round World 1):** 16 nodes in `W01/Gathering`, four clusters of four: oak groves on the west side north of the pond (x −122..−78, z 30..125) and the east side south of it (x 78..122, z −165..−70), rock outcrops opposite each (east north, west south). Placed with `ServerStorage.DevTools.Replant` (`R.fill` per cluster region, `tree` / `solid`, grass only), so they keep the decorations' clearances (paths, water, buildings, enemy wander discs, the boss sightline) and overlap nothing. Named `OakTree_1..8`, `Boulder_1..8`. The first plaza test copies are gone; backups in `ServerStorage/MapBackup_2026-10-19` (`GatherPlaceholders`, `W1GatheringBeforeScatter`). **Breaking stays per player:** the server never changes a node's parts (checked 2026-10-19: chopped on one client, still standing on the server), so other players never see someone else's broken node. Playtested: 5 chops → stump + 3 Oak Logs, 6 hits → rubble + 3 Stone, clean console. The material uses are still to do.
  - **World 1's decorations moved off the nodes (2026-10-20, the user: trees and rocks "really close" to the oaks and boulders, which were placed over the existing decorations):** 22 decorations whose bounding box came within 4 studs of a standing node (6 trees, 1 rock, 15 bushes, most overlapping) went to `ServerStorage/MapBackup_2026-10-20/W1DecorMovedFromNodes` (`WasAt`, `WasIn`), and a copy of each was replanted with `DevTools.Replant` (`R.fill` with the piece itself as the template, its own kind folder, every decoration folder in `spaceAll`; Replant already reserves each node's box grown 3). One blossom took two more tries (its petal ring hung over a step, then it stood within 1 stud of a plaza connector) and stands at (25, −4); the petal sources were rebuilt (11). Checked: every moved piece on its ground, no decoration within 3 studs of a node, counts unchanged (trees 56, rocks 26, bushes 118). An older `Rock_Boulder_A` by the arena path was nudged 1 stud to (−12.6, −207.4) (the user's yes; the original is in the same backup), so no solid decoration now stands within 1 stud of a path, the plaza or the arena.

Templates `ReplicatedStorage/Assets/Tools/StoneAxe` / `StoneRod` like the wooden ones (invisible `Grip` PrimaryPart, the rod's `Tip` at (0, 0.03, −4.68), every mesh welded at its grip-space offset, `ToolId`, `Handed` 1); holds = the wooden ones'. `Config/Tools`: tier 2, World 2, Uncommon, power 20 (axe), placeholder prices 600 / 800 and sell 120 / 160. Weaponsmith rows `Item_StoneAxe` / `Item_StoneRod` (World 2's Tools tab, Uncommon skin). **Icons** like World 1's: `w1_icons_rerender` `render(id)` (the rod's thin `Rod` and `Stone` pieces without ink, or the stone shaft came out black) finished by `art/tools/ui/tool_icons.py StoneAxe StoneRod` → `art/icons/tools/` (axe `rbxassetid://138518954912606`, rod `115528900023286`). ShopUI makes the cards once and refreshes counts on `Materials`, `OwnedTools` and `Coins`; Buy stays locked until all are met; the grid hides once owned. Playtested: an Ore Rock broke in 6 Crystal Pickaxe hits into its rubble (3 Iron Ore), a cluster in 5 (shards), the Crystal Rod's cast into the lake accepted and a Glowfish caught through the three rings; clean console; the test tools and materials were taken back (the Index still counts them as found).

- **Fishing (2026-10-18, the user's design; rules and numbers in `Shared.Config.Fishing`).** The rod is a gathering tool (`Tools.WoodenRod`, kind `Rod`, `handed = 2`, swing `Cast`); fish are materials with `fished = true` and `source` = the fishing spot (`Index.materialSource` reads `Fishing.chanceAt`). World 1: `Minnow` (Common, Easy, weight 60), `Sunfish` (Uncommon, Medium, 30), `GoldenCarp` (Rare, Hard, 10) at spot `SunnyPond`.
  - **Tools work only in their own world or an older one (the user, 2026-10-20):** a fishing spot has a rod `tier` (`Config/Fishing`, `SunnyPond` 1, `RedwoodPond` 2) like a gathering node's `tier`. A weaker rod's cast is refused by `FishingService` and, on the client, splashes down and comes back with "Needs a Stone Rod" (`Fishing.rodFor(spot)`, the lowest-tier rod that can fish it), as a weak axe on a tree gets "Needs a Stone Axe". A better rod still fishes older water.
  - **World 2 fishing (2026-10-20):** World 2's single water part (`W02…/Ground/Creek/Water/Water`, the creek and pond) is tagged `FishingWater` with `Spot = "RedwoodPond"` ("The Creek", World 2, tier 2): `Trout` (Common, Easy, weight 60, sell 6), `Perch` (Uncommon, Medium, 30, sell 11; added the same day, the user: three tiers like World 1's pond) and `RainbowTrout` (Rare, Hard, 10, sell 18), materials with `fished = true` and `source = "RedwoodPond"`. Icons drawn in World 1's fish shape (`art/tools/icons/w2_fish_icons.html` → `svg_render.py` → `finish_w2_fish.py` → `art/icons/materials/`): Trout `rbxassetid://93149400962088` (olive, gold belly, dark spots), Rainbow Trout `112485612041250` (silvery green, pink stripe, speckles, a sparkle), Perch `88894631856595` (yellow-green, four dark bars, a spiny orange dorsal fin and orange fins). Playtested: the Wooden Rod at World 2's pond pops "Needs a Stone Rod" and a forced cast is refused ("Reeled"); the Stone Rod's cast is accepted (Cast → Approach → Bite) and a hooked fish through three rings landed a Trout in the materials; clean console. World 2's fish feed its three potions (below).
  - **Water:** every `W01…/Ground/River/Channel/Water` part (the pond slab and the stream's steps) is tagged `FishingWater` with `Spot = "SunnyPond"`; anywhere on it works (pier or bank). A new water part needs the tag and attribute too.
  - **Flow:** a click with the rod out plays the two-handed `Cast` swing (held one-handed by `HOLDS.WoodenRod`; both hands over the head, release at the `Hit` marker). `Client.Controllers.FishingController` flies the bobber 20 studs ahead in an arc (an Animation.Scheduler Render system, priority 40) and draws the line as a Beam from the rod Grip's `Tip` attachment (0, 0.03, −4.68) to the bobber's `Line` attachment, through two Terrain attachments so it sags downward. Over water it sends `Remotes.FishAction { action = "cast", at }`. `Server.Services.FishingService` checks the owned rod in hand, range and water, then sends `Remotes.FishState`: `Approach` (the shadow swims in), `Bite` (bobber under for 1–2 s; a click sends `hook`), `Surface` (missed, wait again), `Ring` { n, delay, closeSeconds, zone } ×3, `Caught` { fish } (`MaterialService.drop`) or `Escaped` (a miss lets the fish go), `Reeled` (asked, rod put away, died, or > `lineBreak` studs away). The server re-checks each ring click's timing (`Fishing.zoneTimes` + `ringSlack`). **After a hit the yellow band and the clicked closer stay on screen for `Fishing.ringGap` (0.5 s, user 2026-10-19: the rings came too fast) before the next ring starts closing**; `FishingRingUI.ring` only resets the band when the ring actually starts.
  - **Clicks:** `WeaponController.setClickFilter` lets FishingController take clicks while a line is out (hook, rings, reel in); only a click with no line out becomes the cast swing. `WeaponController.playExtra(name, seconds)` plays the rod's `Reel` swing on landing (`WeaponClips.extra(def, name)` builds any SWINGS entry with a def's hold). On a catch the one `FishShadow` model leaps out to over the player's head, spins and pops (`DeathPop`); `Splash` is a new EffectPreset.
  - **Models:** `ReplicatedStorage/Assets/Fishing/Bobber` (PrimaryPart `Root`, `Line` attachment at y 0.68) and `FishShadow` (one dark model for every fish; only icons differ, user's rule). Blender `w1_fishing_build` (collection `W1_Fishing`) → `art/meshes/w1_fishing.fbx` + offsets: Bobber `Red` (top + stem) / `White`, FishShadow `Body` (1f2a36, its own PrimaryPart: FishingController changes every part's transparency, so it has no hidden root). Meshes swapped in 2026-10-18 (red d8342c / white f4f1ea, SmoothPlastic, no collision). **In the water the fish is that model rolled on its side and pressed flat** (`SHADOW_FLAT` 0.08 of its width) 0.1 over the surface, solid: the pond water (0.25 transparent) hides anything under it, and two see-through surfaces washed it out. It swims in from open water (`openWaterNear`, never from under a bank) and gets its full size back to leap out on a catch.

Gui attributes `PipIdle` / `PipDone` / `PipMiss`, `ZoneIdle` / `ZoneHit` / `ZoneMiss`, `PopSeconds`, `FinishSeconds`. Playtested: rings close, zone resizes per fish (Easy 0.32 / Medium 0.26 / Hard 0.2), the gui pops away after the catch.
- **Infuser reveal has priority over the altar display (2026-10-19).** `InfuserController` shows each player's infused cores and current Worldheart tier on every altar, but a new core's attribute waits `REVEAL_WAIT` (0.6 s) before the displays refresh, the reveal clears its altar's display, raises the current tier, turns it into the next tier (`Infuse.bladeOf`) in the flash, and the displays come back when it ends. **Server code that looks up tagged models must skip copies outside Workspace:** backups in ServerStorage keep their tags (9 `Infuser`s there), and `InfuseService.infuserNear` picked a backup, so the client got `infuser = nil` and only the display changed.
- **Potions (2026-10-19, the user's design; plan in the `potion-plan` memory; all numbers in `Shared.Config.Potions`).** Four potions, three tiers each (id `<Kind><tier>`, "Strength2"): **Health** heals 30 / 50 / 80% of max HP over `HEAL_SECONDS` (20 s `HEALTH_COOLDOWN`, refused at full health); **Strength** +15 / 25 / 40% weapon damage (`MeleeService`, applied last); **Stoneskin** −10 / 20 / 30% damage taken (after armour's defence); **Swiftness** +10 / 18 / 25% walk and sprint speed (`SprintController` now sets WalkSpeed every step from `StarterPlayer.CharacterWalkSpeed` × potion × sprint). Timed ones last 3 / 4 / 5 min. Tiers follow world bands (`Potions.Bands`: I = Worlds 1–2, II = 3–5, III = 6–7); a Crafter brews only its band's tier, and Tier II/III recipes take a potion of the tier below. Recipes and coin costs are placeholders; Tier I uses World 1 materials until World 2 has gathering, Tier II World 3's enemy drops, Tier III has none yet. Rarity tiles: I Uncommon, II Epic, III Legendary.
  - **Each Tier I potion belongs to one world (the user, 2026-10-20):** a def's `world` (Config/Potions, Tier I only): Health, Strength, Stoneskin and Swiftness are World 1's, brewed only at its Crafter from World 1 items; Regen, Gatherer's and Angler's are World 2's, brewed only at its Crafter from World 2 items (CraftingUI's `potionsHere`, PotionService's `atCrafterFor`). Gatherer's I takes 4 Redwood Logs (it took Oak Logs). Tier II / III keep the band rule. Playtested: each Crafter lists only its own, and the server refuses a Regen brew at World 1 and a Health brew at World 2.

All last 3 / 4 / 5 min and follow the same rules. Recipes: Regen I 2 Trout + 3 Fox Fur + 1 Amber Sap + 60 coins (the Amber Sap added 2026-10-20, the user: use it in a World 2 recipe; sap heals the tree); Gatherer's I 2 Perch + 2 Boar Tusk + 4 Redwood Log + 75; Angler's I 1 Rainbow Trout + 2 Perch + 1 Bear Claw + 90. Sell 40 / 45 / 55 for Tier I. Each def has a `short` name ("Gatherer's I") for the BoostHud chip and the Crafter tile; `Potions.percent` prints 1.5 without a ".0". Crafter effect card titles Regen ("+1%/s"), Tool Power, Rare Fish. Icons in the same style (`potion_icons.py`: Regen green with a plus `rbxassetid://90761972576074` / `100261071992720` / `96275987831548`, Gatherer's amber with a leaning axe `135167643332794` / `131335149938680` / `105862877284140`, Angler's blue with a fish `102947906879139` / `71152897917402` / `91090191334479`); `potion_icons.py finish Regen Gatherer Angler` re-crops only the named kinds. Playtested: admin give and drink (chips "Regen I", "Gatherer's I", "Angler's I"), heal 1.8% a second with Regen I (2% expected, Roblox's own tick), an oak felled in 4 chops at 12.5, a hooked fish with Angler's running, all three brewed at the World 1 Crafter (1,000 → 775 coins; a second Angler's refused for materials); the Crafter's Potions column holds all seven; clean console.
  - **Rules (`PotionService`):** the same potion and tier adds its time; a higher tier replaces a lower one; a lower tier is refused while a higher one runs ("weaker"); different potions run together. Timers tick only while the player is in game: **dying never touches them and leaving pauses them** (user's rule; saved as the `potions` section with their order). Published: `Potions` ("Health1=2,..."), and per kind `Potion_<kind>` (seconds left), `PotionTier_<kind>`, `PotionOrder_<kind>` (a `ShopService.nextStamp()` stamp), plus `PotionHealReady` (server time). `Potions.multiplier(player, kind)` reads them on either side. Remotes: `BrewPotion(id)` (at a Crafter of the band: `StallService.atStall`) and `DrinkPotion(id)`, both `(ok, reason)`.
  - **Crafter Potions tab** (option on https://claude.ai/artifact/W9XiBtGwqgKrgc5hacFfjn): `CraftingShop/Window/Tabs` (`TabArmor` / `TabPotions`, a copy of the Weaponsmith's, same gui `Tab*` colour attributes). **`Info/Ingredients` is a ScrollingFrame** (2026-10-19, user: Harvest Knight's five rows ran under the Craft button): it ends just above the button, and CraftingUI's `fitRows` keeps each row its `RowShare` (0.3) and each gap its `GapShare` (0.04) of the visible height, setting the canvas from the list's measured height (a ScrollingFrame's CanvasSize scale is a share of its parent, not itself) and refitting on resize. **The `Sets` column is 0.2 of the window (x 0.026) and `Detail` starts at 0.24** (2026-10-20, the user: hovering and selecting potions threw the tiles around; two 0.48-wide tiles plus the gap filled the old 0.15 column, so a grown tile wrapped its row): `PotionTemplate` is 0.4 wide and `SetTemplate` 0.75, the same drawn size as before, so two grown potion tiles still fit side by side. On Potions the `Sets` list holds `Templates/PotionTemplate`s (smaller `SetTemplate` copies; the list's UIListLayout now wraps horizontally, so armour sets still stack one per row) with no Pointer; the detail shows `Info/EffectCard` (title per kind, the potion's icon) and `LastsCard` (hourglass `rbxassetid://113204917863533`, "Instant" for Health) instead of the Defense / Health cards, `Level` reads "Have ×N" (`HaveFormat`) and `Craft` says `TextBrew`.

A refusal shows on `Effect` ("Already at full health", "A stronger one is running", "Ready in 12s").

- **Talkers: NPCs you talk to without a quest (2026-10-18).** A model tagged `Talker` with a `TalkerId` attribute names a `Shared.Config.Talkers` def (`npc` name / short / portrait, and `talk(player)` returning `{ lines, tag, later }`). `QuestController` gives it the same Talk prompt, face shot and Dialogue card as a quest giver, and closes on one button.
  - **Finn the fisherman** (`W01…/Npcs/Fisherman`, `TalkerId = "Fisherman"`): on the fishing hut's landing, left of the door, facing the middle path. Dale's R6 body (skin e0a86f) with Blender gear (`w1_keeper_fisher_build`, `art/meshes/w1_keeper_finn.fbx` + offsets): yellow bucket hat, short white beard on the cylinder head, olive vest over a blue-striped shirt, green waders and boots, and a walking cane (`Cane`, `CaneTip`, `Bone = "Tool"`) in the right hand, arm posed 15° forward, `HandPoint` (1.5, −0.94, −0.45). `IdleStyle = "Farmer"` (the cane arm holds still). **A tutorial only (user's call):** rods are bought from the shopkeeper; with no `Rod` in `OwnedTools` he sends you to buy one, otherwise he explains casting, the bite and the three closing rings. **The card's tag is set with the name and photo before it opens** (`DialogueUI.setSpeaker(name, portrait, tag)`; QuestController's `speaker()` returns the tag): it used to wait for the first line, so going from one NPC to another showed the last one's "Quest 3 of 10" / "Fishing Lesson" for a moment (user, 2026-10-19). Portrait `rbxassetid://136031819587344` (2026-10-19, `art/portraits/quest/portrait_Finn.png`, shot like the quest givers' from a little below the brim so his eyes show).

Tools upgrade from the previous tier of the same tool plus the new world's materials; a world without an upgrade for a kind leaves the old tool to the next one that has it (World 2 work).
- **Questlines, one per world (lines run side by side; each player has a step in every line).** A giver is a model tagged `QuestGiver` (plus `AmbientIdle`) whose `QuestLine` attribute names its line; adding a line = `lines.<Id>` + `Quests.order` + a giver. Full checklist: the `add-questline` skill.

  | Line | World | Giver | Levels | Step 8 item | Step 10 weapon |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | `DaleFarm` "Trouble on Dale's Farm" | 1 | Dale (`W01…/Npcs/Farmer`) | 9 / 14 | LuckyHorseshoe | WeathervaneSpear |
  | `RangerRedwoods` "Trouble in the Redwoods" | 2 | Ranger Rowan (`W02…/Npcs/Ranger`) | 30 / 34 | RangerBadge | LanternFlail |
