- **Bonuses add, and every screen but the Index shows your real numbers (2026-10-23, the user: multiplied, maxed luck reached 16.5x; "all the stats and their multipliers should appear over all UIs").** `Shared.Config.Bonuses` works out each stat's total for the server (the real effect) and every screen (the numbers shown): every source gives a share (x1.5 = +0.5) and a stat is 1 + their sum. Luck / Coins / EXP = 1 + the shop's passes and boosts (ShopService now sums their shares into `LuckBoost` / `CoinBoost` / `ExpBoost`) + the upgrade's share (`Bonuses.luck` / `coins` / `exp`, read by IndexService, MaterialService, CoinService, ExpService and IndexUI's shown chances); damage = 1 + Strength + Attack upgrade, times the admin's `DamageMultiplier` test tool on top (`Bonuses.damage`, MeleeService); speed = 1 + min(Swiftness + armour speed, `Armor.MAX_SPEED`) + the Sprint upgrade's share while sprinting (`Bonuses.speed`, SprintController); max health x the Health upgrade (`Bonuses.health`). **Damage taken still multiplies** (armour, Stoneskin, the Defense upgrade: added, the reductions would reach 98%). Maxed today: luck 4.95x (was 16.5x), coins / EXP 3.71x (7.35x), damage 2.12x (2.41x). `Bonuses.weapon(player, def)` is a weapon as the player swings it (Worldheart tier, Attack Speed upgrade, damage bonuses) and `Bonuses.dps` its damage a second (hits only, no crits: the user's pick). They fill the Inventory's tile pills, sorting, Equip Best, `WeaponStats` card and the Damage / Health / Defense mini cards (Defense is now the whole reduction: armour x Stoneskin x Defense upgrade), the Weaponsmith's chips (filled in `ShopUI.refresh` now, not once), the Crafter's Health card (x the Health upgrade) and the Stats panel (each chip shows its share as an added multiplier, the user's rule, 2026-10-23: "Passes & boosts +1.5×" on a ×2.50 total, "Upgrade +0.15×", "Armor +0.05×"; only Crit Chance, Regen and Gathering stay percentages). **The Index keeps the base numbers** (the user: so players know a weapon's or set's own stats): `WeaponTile.dps(def, true)`. Screens refresh on `Bonuses.Watch` (every attribute a shown stat reads). A new gamepass or boost that raises a stat adds its share in `Bonuses` (or in `Config/Shop` for luck / coins / EXP). **Bug fixed 2026-10-24 (the user: the Index showed almost nothing):** every shared tile worked out a DPS for its pill even when the pill was hidden, and with any Attack Speed upgrade `Upgrades.adjust` multiplied a missing `cooldown` on a material, fish, potion or treasure, so the Index stopped drawing its five item tabs and a boss's drop slip (it never showed in testing because the test saves had Attack Speed at 0). Now `Upgrades.adjust` passes anything without a swing through untouched, and `WeaponTile.fill` works out the DPS only for a pill that shows it. Playtested at Attack Speed 24: every tab full (12 / 27 / 6 / 5 / 16 / 6 / 11 / 3), the Scarecrow King's slip, the Inventory; clean console. Playtested: 2x Luck + 1.5x Luck + VIP = LuckBoost 2.75, with Luck 3 2.90 (the Stats panel's chips +175% / +15%); a Dagger with Attack 3 and Strength I hits 7.44 for 18.6 DPS in the Inventory; the Sickle's Weaponsmith row 15.6 -> 20.2 DPS with Attack Speed 3 too while its Index slip stays 15.6; Health 3 gives 115 max HP; clean console. **The Health icon was re-centred** (its heart sat high: the lobes carry its weight, so it was moved 20 px down onto its shadow, which was redrawn): `rbxassetid://104320402035577` (was `82838416987322`), swapped in `Config/Upgrades` and 9 images in Studio.
- **Stats panel (2026-10-19, option A "stat sheet" on https://claude.ai/artifact/E7RQQXPYH2ZCKBB6V4YaWk; plan in the `stats-leaderboards-plan` memory):** `WeaponHud/SideButtons/StatsButton` (the clipboard, `rbxassetid://90361466146096`, `art/tools/icons/hud_stats_ranks.html` -> `art/ui/hud/hudB_stats.png`) opens `StarterGui/StatsPanel` (`Panel` = "Stats"), wired by `Client.UI.StatsUI`. Every stat is a **total multiplier**, never a percentage (the user's rule), with a chip per source: Max Health (the number: level HP + armour, x the Health upgrade), Luck / Coins / EXP (`LuckBoost` / `CoinBoost` / `ExpBoost` x the upgrade), Damage (Strength x upgrade), Defense (1 / armour's damage x Stoneskin x upgrade), Attack Speed, Regen (the percentage of max HP a second: base 1%, the upgrade's share, a Regen potion's; a percentage since 2026-10-22), Sprint (upgrade x Swiftness; "Locked" before Sprint 1), Gathering, and since 2026-10-22 Crit Damage (the crit multiplier: chips "Base ×1.50", "Upgrade +0.10") and Crit Chance (a percentage: "Base 10% of hits", "Upgrade +3%", total 13%), Gathering (the chance to double: "Chance to double drops", "Upgrade +3%"), the third row (12 rows: the grid's cell is 0.152 tall with 0.0176 gaps, was 0.183 / 0.02 for 10). `Window/Sheet` is a 2-column UIGridLayout of `Templates/StatRow` clones (Tile/Icon, StatName, Parts of `Templates/PartChip`, Total; `UpColor` / `SameColor`); it refreshes every 0.5 s while open. Flat bonuses come first, then multipliers (Max Health is the only stat with one). An admin's own test settings show as an "Admin" chip only while set: "Admin ×10.00" on Damage (`DamageMultiplier`), "Admin override" on Max Health (`MaxHealthOverride`, which replaces the whole max). Playtested: Luck x3.45 from a 3x boost and Luck 3, Defense x1.26 from 18% armour and Defense 3, Regen 2.45% with Regen 3 and a Regen I potion, Gathering 3% (re-checked 2026-10-22; the forced-100% test doubled an oak's 3 logs to 6).
- **Player card and press-E inspect (2026-10-23, the user's picks on https://claude.ai/artifact/MkJ7Xobg1XbSe6zZ8gJxfp: A "profile sheet" for the card, C "peek" for inspecting).** Both read only the shown player's published attributes (Player attributes replicate to every client), so nothing new runs on the server. **`Client.UI.ProfileUI`** fills hand-built `StarterGui/PlayerProfile` (`Panel` = "Profile", a copy of the Stats panel's window; its `Title` is the player's display name): your own card opens from your avatar on the HUD card (`WeaponHud/.../AvatarHolder/ProfileButton`, an invisible button), anyone's from a player list row (`PlayerList/Templates/Row/Hit`) or the inspect card. `Tabs/Tab<Stats|Inventory|Trophies>` (pinned pills, the Ranks panel's period colours as gui `Pill*` attributes, the open one `Selected`). `Identity` (always shown): `Avatar/Photo` (the headshot), `Avatar/LevelBadge` (the HUD badge's copy, kept centred by `PlayerCardUI.watchBadge`), `PlayerName`, `UserName` "@name", `TitleChip` (the worn title or "No title yet"), `Medal` (the player list's chip), `Health` / `XP` (the HUD bars' copies), `CoinsTile` / `IndexTile` (Index progress over every world), `WorldLine`. Stats tab: `Gear/WeaponCard` and `ArmorCard` (red / blue heads; `TileSlot` with the shared tile, `ItemName`, `Rarity` in the gui's `Color_<Rarity>`, `Stat/Value`: the weapon's DPS with THAT player's bonuses (`WeaponTile.dps(def, base, who)`), the set's defence, its health after their Health upgrade), `Gear/ViewInventory` (opens the Inventory tab), and `Stats/Sheet` (a 2-column grid of `Templates/StatLine`: `Icon`, `StatName`, `Total` in `UpColor` / `SameColor`). Inventory tab: `InventoryView/Grid` (the Inventory's square-cell grid) of read-only shared tiles, one per thing for the session (weapons by their DPS, then armour, tools, potions, materials), `Count` (`Format` "%s items"), `Empty`. Trophies tab: `TrophiesView`, the Ranks panel's shelf (kept at its 2.09 shape), filled by `LeaderboardUI.fillTrophies(box, who)` ("Peng's trophies" for someone else). **Layout fixes (2026-10-23, the user's screenshots):** `Tabs` is a copy of the Ranks panel's `Periods` group (the user: "exactly like the Ranks tab"; the same size and spot, its UIListLayout and padding, buttons renamed `Tab<Stats|Inventory|Trophies>`), so a selected pill stays inside the dark bar; with no armour worn, `ItemName` ("No armor") moves to the middle of the card below its head (its authored spot is kept in `BasePosition` / `BaseAnchor`), on both cards; `Health` / `XP` sit at 0.618 / 0.703 of `Identity`, a gap like the HUD card's `BarGap`; each gear card's `ItemName`, `Rarity` and `Stat` are centred in the space right of the tile (x 0.73, 0.46 wide; `Stat` is a `CenterRow` of its icon and number), clear of the tile, and the inspect minis' text is centred beside theirs the same way. Refreshes every 0.5 s while open. **The stat numbers are `Client.Util.StatSheet`** (`measure(who, id)`, `ORDER`, `name`), moved out of StatsUI so the Stats panel and the card always agree. **`Client.UI.InspectUI`:** every other player's character gets a client-side `ProximityPrompt` "Inspect <name>" (E, 10 studs, drawn by PromptController from `Assets/UI/InspectPrompt`, a Talk bubble copy with `TextFormat` "Inspect %s"); it opens hand-built `StarterGui/InspectPeek` (`Panel` = "Inspect": a cream card with `Avatar`, the badge, `PlayerName`, `TitleLine` (gui `TitleFormat` "%s · World %d"), `Health`, `Medal`, `WeaponMini` / `ArmorMini`, `ProfileButton` teal "Full Profile" and `InventoryButton` green, `Close`, `Catcher`, `Blocker`). The two cards share `ProfileUI.fillIdentity` / `fillGear`. **Testing alone in Studio:** set the Studio-only `DevInspectSelf` attribute on your player (Client datamodel) and the prompt goes on your own character. Playtested: the card from the avatar and the player list, all three tabs (57 tiles sorted by DPS), the peek by pressing E, its Inventory button opening the card's Inventory tab; the Stats panel unchanged; clean console. **Two-player test (2026-10-23, Test > Start Test Session > Server & Clients, then Add Clients 2 in the server window; each window is its own MCP Studio instance):** Player1 pressing E on Player2 (level 27, the Mace, Harvest Knight, Attack 6, Luck 3) showed Player2's card: 435 HP, the Mace at 43.7 DPS (37 x 1.18, Player2's bonuses; Player1's own would be 37), Luck x1.15, Damage x1.18, Defense x1.22, 48,250 coins, their 3 items on the Inventory tab; Player2 inspecting Player1 the same way round; a card held open followed the other player's level-up within a second; clean consoles on both clients and the server. Studio's test players have no avatars, so the headshot stays blank there. (The MCP mouse tool's `instance_path` clicks miss buttons near the top of the screen by the 58 px top bar; click at the button's AbsolutePosition centre instead.)

Words and colours are attributes on `Assets/UI/DamagePopup` (`HealFormat`, `CooldownFormat`, `HealHitColor`, `InfoHitColor`; `DamagePopup.show` kinds `Heal` / `Info`). - Avoid z-fighting: overlapping parts must not share a face plane; make one slightly larger or offset it.
- **Optimisation pass (2026-10-19, user's request):** all 1,982 walk-through MeshParts in World 1, and every walk-through mesh in `ServerStorage.Assets` / `ReplicatedStorage.Assets`, now use `CollisionFidelity = Box` (no visual change; detailed collision for parts nothing collides with was wasted memory and build time). 4 stray `Plastic` parts became SmoothPlastic (the cellar portal's `ForceField` swirl is intentional). `MapOptimizer.merge` removed 26 more blocks (PathDetails 9, LowGround 2, Dirt 12, Structures 3); `remesh` found nothing left. Every one of the 92 invisible parts has a job (enemy roots and mood faces, particle sources, sound anchors, zones, shop rings). **Trims rebuilt** with `DevTools.PathTrims` (204 strips; only the two bridges cut them now; the strip in front of Dale had a stale 1.5-stud gap). **Flicker fix:** 13 stepping stones that overlapped each other were removed, and 11 hedge-top `Grass` blocks raised 0.02 above the `Dirt` beside them (they shared a top face); a coplanar-overlap scan of all ground, hills and hedges now finds none. Backups in `ServerStorage/MapBackup_2026-10-19` (`W1TrimsBefore`, `W1SteppingStonesBefore`, `W1OuterRimBefore`, `W1GroundBeforeMerge`, `W1StructuresBeforeMerge`; their tags moved into `_BackupTags`).
- **Performance pass, Worlds 1 and 2 (2026-10-21, the user's request; greedy merging included):** World 1 5,368 → 5,148 parts, World 2 4,172 → 4,165. **History markers blocked merging:** `MapOptimizer.merge` skips any block with an attribute, and 983 ground blocks carried markers only ever written, never read (`BlockRegen` 558, `WidenStrip` 369, `LoopHill` 49, `HoleFill` 6), so the earlier passes found nothing; they were dropped (the history stays in these notes), then merging per parent and name (a `Grass` never swallows a `Path` or a `Step`) took out 170 blocks in World 1 (`Dirt` 116, `LowGround` 37, `GrassStep` 16, one elevated `Grass`) and 1 in World 2. **Don't mark ground blocks with history attributes;** write it here instead. `MapOptimizer.buried` found 56 blocks entirely inside a cliff block, a hedge or a bridge plank (World 1's `Dirt` / `Grass` / `GrassStep` under the `RimBlock`s, World 2's hill steps under `Hedge_7/18/19/26/27`, a stepping stone in `RightPathBridge`'s planks); removed, copies in `ServerStorage/MapBackup_2026-10-21/BuriedPartsRemoved_2026-10-21` (`WasIn`). `remesh` gains nothing in World 1 (its grids pass the cell cap) and only 15 parts in World 2, so it wasn't run. **Every walk-through MeshPart** in both worlds and `Workspace.Shared` now uses `CollisionFidelity = Box` (2,058: World 2's 1,952 had never been switched, World 1 had 84 new ones from the stalls and props since 2026-10-19; World 3's 1,040 wait for its remodel); in bulk this takes Studio over the tool timeout, but it finishes. The 25 `Plastic` parts (all invisible: zones, particle sources, critter roots, rail guards, the World 3 gate's `Glow`) are SmoothPlastic. Checked: a 2-stud height-and-colour map of both worlds' ground identical before and after (the one changed row was the stone inside the bridge planks), all 60 World 1 / 2 enemies on their ground in a playtest, clean console. Backups `MapBackup_2026-10-21/W1GroundBeforeMerge_2026-10-21`, `W2GrassBeforeMerge_2026-10-21` (untagged).
- **Part-count tool:** `ServerStorage.DevTools.MapOptimizer` is a dev-only module; load it in Edit mode with `loadstring(...Source)()`. It provides:
  - `merge`: exact greedy merges.
  - `remesh`: rebuilds each look of world-aligned blocks as the fewest boxes, running through buried space only, so nothing visible changes.
  - `buried`: parts hidden inside another solid block.
  - `nudgeClear`: slides a decoration out of whatever it's stuck in.

  World 1 was optimized with it on 2026-10-07.
- **Blossom trees on mounds (2026-10-08, user's report):** each `Tree_Blossom` has a flat `FallenPetals` ring (about 6×7) that floated where a tree stood near a mound tier's edge. 7 of the 8 mound trees were fixed.
  - Three were turned and shifted under 1.5 studs.
  - One moved 5 studs along its tier.
  - One moved up onto Hill_R02's top tier.
  - On the crowded Hill_R01 / Hill_L02 tiers, two trees stayed put and only their `FallenPetals` were slid (one also shrunk to 80%).
  - Growing the mound blocks was rejected: it would bury ledge fences, hit the house and break path/plaza clearance.
  - **Check:** sample an 11×11 grid under each `FallenPetals` and raycast down onto `Ground` / `Hills`. Every hit must be at the ring's own height.
- Decorations rest on `Ground` (its `Hills` included) but shouldn't push into anything else. A flower or tuft stuck in a rock or wall is wasted parts.
- **Keep every decoration at least 1 stud from the paths, the plaza and the boss arena** (`Ground/Paths`, `Ground/Hub`, `Ground/BossArena`), the user's rule from 2026-10-07.
  - Bridges are the exception, and so are the map's base pieces that border the plaza (the `Ground/Rim` blocks and `Hill_R01`).
  - Two bushes in narrow strips between the cliff and the plaza were scaled to 70–80% to fit.
  - Check: grow each path part by 1 stud on each side, covering from 0.5 below its top to 2.5 above, and look for decoration parts inside.
  - On 2026-10-07, 102 items were moved out this way (grass, flowers, bushes, a boulder). None were removed.
- **Keep every decoration at least 1 stud from the pond's open water** (`Ground/River` water not covered by ground), the user's rule from 2026-10-07. That includes the reeds; lily pads are the only exception.
  - Check:
    1. Raycast a 0.5-stud grid over each water part; cells where water is the first `Ground` hit are open water.
    2. Merge each row into strips, and grow every strip 1 stud on all sides, from 0.5 under the water surface to 10 above it.
    3. Look for decoration parts inside the strips.
  - The 10-stud height catches tree canopies hanging over the water.
  - On 2026-10-07 this moved 18 reed clumps, 9 grass tufts, 7 flower patches, 4 rocks (2 shrunk slightly) and 1 oak. Bridges, rim hedges and hills are map base and stayed.

## Skills

The skills are **personal skills** in `~/.claude/skills/` (not in this repo), so they work in any session:
- `add-enemy` and `animate-enemy`: enemies.
- `add-weapon` and `animate-weapon`: weapons (one- and two-handed).
- `animate-player`: idle, walk and sprint (`Clips.Locomotion`) and previewing R6 poses.
- `roblox-part-modeling`: building and redesigning any part-built model, with the preview-and-approve workflow.
- `blender-modeling`: blocky meshes made in Blender (Blender MCP), exported as FBX and set up in Studio.
- `roblox-ui-icons`: drawing UI icons as PNGs, uploading them, and swapping them into hand-built UI. Icon sources live in `art/`.
- `roblox-ui-text-centering`: centring text and icon + text groups on buttons, badges and bubbles. It covers Luckiest Guy's measured 0.16 × TextSize lift, face vs lip, fixed-pixel 9-slice insets and scaled screenshots, and measuring with `art/ui_center_check.py`. Use it before calling any label centred.

Use them for those jobs, and update them when a job teaches something new.

## Testing in Studio

- There are no automatic test suites any more (`LogicTests` / `CombatSim` tested the deleted dice duel). After a change, start a playtest and check `get_console_output` for errors and warnings, then exercise the feature for real (kill an enemy, talk to Dale…).
- Each `execute_luau` call runs in its own Luau VM: `require` gives a **fresh** module instance, so you can't read or call a running service's state. Drive the game through remotes, attributes and instances instead.
- Tool calls take seconds, so an enemy keeps fighting between steps. For combat tests, raise the player's `MaxHealth`/`Health` from the **Server** datamodel (client-side changes don't count).
- Screenshots of poses mislead; when it matters, measure directions numerically (e.g. a part's `LookVector` relative to `HumanoidRootPart`).
- After a scripted camera shot, set `workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType` back (Custom in play, Fixed in edit). Stop the playtest when done.
